# Household hh_s5 — 42 days, Monday to Sunday

Seed-generated household. Times are clock times; ids are the receptacle and object ids used in events.jsonl. A line indented under an activity says where an object went when the activity ended and why. WHIM marks a placement that landed somewhere other than where the decision was heading. Text in [brackets] names the hidden cause the decision is attributed to.

## Household

Type: couple. Rooms: bathroom, bedroom_1, dining, entry, kitchen, living.

Residents:

- **Sam** (resident_1): works outside the home; fairly tidy (tidiness 0.66); punctual (jitter scale 0.59); rarely forgets pocket items; mood strongly affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.99); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the bedroom_1 desk. Hobbies: board_games, meditation, reading_living. Chores they take on: fixing, grocery_run, ironing.
- **Yuki** (resident_2): works outside the home; fairly tidy (tidiness 0.67); punctual (jitter scale 0.77); rarely forgets pocket items; mood strongly affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.84); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the bedroom_1 desk. Hobbies: gym, plants, snacking. Chores they take on: dusting, grocery_run, ironing, vacuuming.

Pet: a dog, mainly looked after by Sam.

## Day 19 — Saturday

**Active causes**

- `ep:sore_back:resident_1:d19`: Sam has a sore back today and sits in the armchair instead of the couch, so things land on the side table (06:30–24:00).
- `sick_day:resident_1` (Sam): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `sick_day:resident_2` (Yuki): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `low_energy:resident_1`: Sam has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
- `low_energy:resident_2`: Yuki has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

## Day 20 — Sunday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_1:d20`: Sam hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:15–15:49).
- `ep:on_a_roll:resident_2:d20`: Yuki is on a roll with work: sessions run long, breaks are short, the mug stays on the desk (13:33–16:56).
- `ep:restless_evening:resident_1:d20`: Sam can't settle this evening and wanders between rooms with things in hand (20:25–24:00).
- `sick_day:resident_1` (Sam): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `sick_day:resident_2` (Yuki): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `low_energy:resident_1`: Sam has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
- `low_energy:resident_2`: Yuki has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

Internal states (0–1): Sam energy 0.00, hurriedness 0.00, distraction 0.39; Yuki energy 0.00, hurriedness 0.00, distraction 0.68

## Day 34 — Sunday

**Active causes**

- `ep:on_a_roll:resident_1:d34`: Sam is on a roll with work: sessions run long, breaks are short, the mug stays on the desk (14:56–18:20).
- `ep:sore_back:resident_1:d34`: Sam has a sore back today and sits in the armchair instead of the couch, so things land on the side table (06:30–24:00).

Internal states (0–1): Sam energy 0.57, hurriedness 0.33, distraction 0.39; Yuki energy 0.29, hurriedness 0.21, distraction 0.50

## Day 36 — Tuesday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_2:d36`: Yuki hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:40–16:23).
- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_1:d36`: Sam is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (15:01–15:31).
- `ep:deadline:resident_2:d36:looming`: Yuki has a deadline tomorrow at noon: works into the evening, eats fast with the laptop at the table (18:00–24:00).
- `ep:on_a_roll:resident_2:d36`: Yuki is on a roll with work: sessions run long, breaks are short, the mug stays on the desk (14:24–17:39).
